## Friday, August 28, 2026 Control-170 strict read-only control + daytrading cron dispatch fix (Controls 170-172)

Control-170 at ~15:01 UTC Aug 28 (Aug 28 17:01 +02). Market OPEN (opened 13:30 UTC). Strict GET-only audit found a critical reliability defect: **zero `daytrading_cron` runs logged on Aug 28** despite market being open for 90+ minutes (last daytrading run was Aug 27 21:56 UTC, all MARKET_CLOSED). Three fixes deployed across Controls 170-172:

**Fix 1 (Control-170):** Daytrading was the only strategy still running `reconcileBrokerOrders` inline at the start of each cycle, while crypto and swing already deferred to the `*/10` maintenance cron. This made daytrading the heaviest invocation by subrequest/D1 budget and the first to silently throw under accumulating D1 state pressure. Fix defers daytrading reconciliation to maintenance using the exact same `RECONCILIATION_DEFERRED_TO_MAINTENANCE` skip pattern already proven in crypto (`src/crypto-strategy.ts:181`) and swing (`src/swing-strategy.ts:135`). Also: schema gate `positionsStrategySchemaReady` now fail-open on transient D1 errors (fail-closed caused silent invocation loss because the fallback `logSchemaBlockedRun` also requires D1). Also: lease acquisition errors now caught with direct D1 INSERT (bypassing Database class schema init that re-throws the same rejected Promise).

**Fix 2 (Control-171):** Batched `pragma_table_info` queries in `ensureTradeLifecycleColumns` to cut schema init D1 round-trips from 14+ to 1. Reduced cycle lease TTL from 10min to 5min to match cron interval.

**Fix 3 (Control-172):** Cloudflare's cron dispatch system silently stopped sending the `*/5 13-21 * * 1-5` cron after an `exceededResources` streak (13:07-14:30 UTC). Re-PUTting the same or equivalent expressions (range, explicit hours, broad `*/5 * * * 1-5`) did not revive it. The fix that worked: switching to `1-59/5 * * * *` (every 5 minutes, offset by 1 to avoid overlapping with `*/10` reconcile). The code's internal `MARKET_CLOSED` check gates execution to market hours (13:30-20:00 UTC), so the broader cron only adds skip runs outside market hours. **First daytrading run (4139) logged at 17:17 UTC** — the first since Aug 27 21:56 UTC. Run 4139 showed `RECONCILIATION_DEFERRED_TO_MAINTENANCE` (deferral working), `SWING_OWNED_EXCLUDE` (cap bypass fix cc9e813 exercised for the FIRST TIME), `NO_ENTRY_RISK` (risk controls active), `EQUITY_DIRECTION_FALLBACK`.

## Thursday, August 27, 2026 Control-150 daytrading cap bypass fix - DEPLOY + read-only control

Control-150 at ~20:00 UTC (Aug 27 22:00 +02). Strict GET-only control followed by a targeted cap-enforcement fix.

**Control findings (read-only):** All 8 endpoints 200 (/health, /api/config, /api/dashboard, /api/positions, /api/runs, /api/trades, /api/account, /). Version 2.6.0 aligned across all surfaces. Equity $98,466.46, ACTIVE, not PDT, +$48.75 today (+0.05%). 25 positions ALL swing, 0 unattributed, source=alpaca (broker-authoritative). broker_ledger_synced_until 2026-08-27T19:51:14Z (fresh). Caps 5000/3700/2000 USD unchanged. Four schedules confirmed. Code bc6255d (Control-149 stale-bar fix deployed and working).

**Daytrading is now ACTIVE and trading.** Control-149's stale-bar threshold fix (3→4 intervals, 15→20 min) successfully unblocked daytrading. Trades 724-738 (all strategy="daytrading") executed during the 19:11-19:46 UTC market window. Daytrading bars now pass the 1200s freshness threshold. This is the first active daytrading since the threshold was introduced.

**NEW finding: daytrading cap bypass on swing-held symbols.** The daytrading risk check builds a `positions` array filtered by `strategy === 'daytrading' || (!strategy && !crypto)`. When daytrading buys a swing-held symbol (e.g. RIVN, AVGO), the broker combines them into one position tagged strategy='swing' in D1. The daytrading cap check excludes it, so the daytrading gross exposure is undercounted. Today's session: ~$3,672 in uncounted daytrading exposure on RIVN (3 buys, 175 shares, ~$2,933) and AVGO (1 buy, 2 shares, ~$739). Actual daytrading gross was ~$8,637 vs the $5,000 cap (1.73x). All positions were round-tripped (bought and sold intraday). The bypass is transient but real.

**Run-log gap analysis (Control-144 lesson applied):** 100-run window 3881-3980. Two known historical gaps: 80 min pre-market (3908→3909, 12:11→13:31 UTC) and 178 min market-hours (3932→3933, 14:38→17:36 UTC). One minor 9.6 min gap (3960→3961, 18:56→19:06 UTC, one missed cron run during Workers Paid plan transition). **No new gaps since 19:06 UTC** — cadence continuous for ~55 min through the 20:01 UTC query. Run 3975 (19:46:41 UTC) hit "Fatal: Too many subrequests by single Worker invocation" — AFTER the Workers Paid upgrade (~18:54 UTC). The error was caught and logged (status=error, errors=1), no gap resulted, cadence recovered immediately (run 3976 at 19:51). This single subrequest error post-upgrade needs monitoring — if the Paid plan is active, the 1000-subrequest limit should not be hit by a single daytrading run.

**Fix deployed (cc9e813):** Exclude swing-owned symbols from daytrading BUY decisions. The `swingOwnedSymbols` set (already computed for the final sync) is now computed early in the cycle and used as a BUY exclusion gate. SELL decisions are never blocked (exits are always allowed). The final sync reuses the early-computed set, saving one DB call. New skip code: `SWING_OWNED_EXCLUDE`. 224 tests / 845 assertions pass, typecheck clean, diff-check clean. Caps 5000/3700/2000 unchanged.

**Known DEGRADED conditions (unchanged):** 3 null-strategy trades persistent (703 PLD, 648 NOW, 645 DUK, all strategy=None). Crypto fail-closed: no rawEdgeBps producer in source (type-only at technical-analysis.ts:50, pass-through at crypto-strategy.ts:29/66, config-sourced at risk-manager.ts:192/195 — never computed from market data or confidence). MATICUSD empty, AVAXUSD stale 79684s (~22h). Fee telemetry asOf Aug 19 (cryptoFeeTelemetryStatus=unavailable). All 100 filled trades: gross/fee/net=null, accounting_status=filled_lot_exact_unavailable (conservative). GitHub PAT (github_pat) not in vault — docs push blocked, docs HEAD local only.

## August 26, 2026 Control-117 read-only control + swing cap bypass fix (auto-reconcile path) + deploy

Control-117 at ~14:00 UTC (Aug 26 16:00 +02). Strict GET-only production control, then code fix and deploy. All six endpoints (`/health`, `/api/config`, `/api/dashboard`, `/api/positions`, `/api/runs`, `/api/trades`) returned HTTP 200. No trigger, submit, cancel, close, replace, retry, migration, or broker-mutating endpoint was called during the control phase.

**Version identity (all aligned):** `/health`=2.6.0, `release_version`=2.6.0, `config.version`=2.6.0. Code `22b3dba` (new — Control-117 fix). Prior code `e89c786` (Control-104). Docs HEAD: this commit (local only — push BLOCKED: github_pat not in vault). 223 tests / 841 assertions, typecheck genuinely clean. Caps 5000/3700/2000 USD unchanged.

**Critical discovery — second swing cap bypass path (Control-101 fix incomplete):** Control-101 (a206690) fixed the final sync path only. Control-117 found that the `BROKER_ONLY_RECONCILED` auto-reconcile path (src/index.ts:567) imports new broker positions WITHOUT a strategy field when swing BUYs fill at market open. The final sync then re-tagged them as 'daytrading' because `swingOwnedSymbols` only looked at D1 positions with `strategy='swing'` — which the newly filled swing positions didn't have yet. Live evidence: the 13 pending swing BUYs (trades 707-719) filled at the 13:30 UTC market open. Runs 3703-3705 show `BROKER_ONLY_RECONCILED` with 13 new positions. By 14:00 UTC, all 13 were tagged `strategy='daytrading'` in /api/positions, making swing_cron see $0 swing exposure — the same bypass as Control-101 but through a different code path.

**Fix (commit 22b3dba):** Added `getSwingTradeSymbols()` to `src/database.ts` — queries the trades table for distinct swing BUY tickers. Merged that set into the exclusion in three paths: (1) `BROKER_ONLY_RECONCILED` auto-reconcile upsert (src/index.ts:567), (2) final sync upsert (src/index.ts:1080), (3) final sync close logic (src/index.ts:1110). This prevents swing fills from being imported as daytrading, re-tagged as daytrading, or closed by the daytrading sync. 3 new regression tests in `test/swing-cap-bypass-recon.test.ts`.

**Deploy:** Two direct Cloudflare API PUT deploys (deploy-1 at 14:05 UTC, deployment_id `aadc21c1d2f444079ad5f0d70c1a6197`; deploy-2 at 14:15 UTC, deployment_id `c276d4b1874e4ee09b7108ba5b32df23` after close-logic fix). Both bundled from `/workspace/alpaca-trading-bot` (NOT `/workspace`). Post-deploy GET verification: /health=2.6.0, all version surfaces aligned.

**Post-deploy live verification:** Run #3715 (14:11 UTC, deploy-1 code) and #3716 (14:16 UTC, deploy-2 code) confirm the fix works — the 13 swing fills are now `unattributed` (strategy=null) in /api/positions instead of `daytrading`. The swing cap bypass is stopped. The 13 D1 position rows were closed by the deploy-1 close logic (before the close-logic fix in deploy-2); they will be re-created with `strategy='swing'` by swing_cron at 22:00 UTC. Until then, BROKER_ONLY_RECONCILED fires each cycle (noisy but harmless — the positions are excluded from upsert and close). 0 errors, 0 CYCLE_LEASE_HELD across all post-deploy runs.

**Live state at control time (14:00 UTC):** 28 broker-authoritative positions (source=alpaca, observed_at 2026-08-26T14:00:20Z): 15 swing MV $7,943.03, 13 daytrading MV $1,448.94 (all 13 were swing fills mis-tagged daytrading by the auto-reconcile bypass). Equity $98,527.06 (change_today +$2.08, +0.002%), ACTIVE, cash $89,136.78, long_market_value $9,390.28, buying_power $381,120.65. last_equity $98,524.98. 30 runs visible: 0 errors, 0 CYCLE_LEASE_HELD. Daytrading skipped on DAYTRADING_BARS_STALE (bars ~15 min stale, ageSeconds ~915 vs 900 max). Crypto fail-closed (RECONCILIATION_DEFERRED_TO_MAINTENANCE, CRYPTO_BARS_STALE, no rawEdgeBps). Trade 703 (PLD) strategy=null persistent. All 4 trigger filters verified. broker_ledger_synced_until 2026-08-26T13:50:11Z. last_prune_date 2026-08-26.

**Swing cap impact:** The 13 swing BUYs (trades 707-719) filled at 13:30 UTC market open, adding ~$1,449 to swing exposure. Total swing MV is now ~$9,392 (2.54x $3,700 cap). These are pre-existing positions from the pre-fix era — the fix prevents FUTURE bypass but does not retroactively cancel filled orders. The swing_cron at 22:00 UTC will see the correct ~$9,392 exposure and will not place new BUYs beyond the cap.

**Remaining follow-ups:** (1) 13 mis-tagged swing fills need swing_cron at 22:00 UTC to re-tag them as 'swing' in D1. (2) rawEdgeBps producer for crypto BUY admission. (3) D1 Sep 1 enforcement monitoring. (4) github_pat restoration for docs push. (5) Trade 703 strategy=null. (6) Run-log delivery gaps (paid-plan upgrade approved, not executed). (7) Daytrading bar freshness (~15 min stale).

**Status:** HEALTHY (code/deploy, fix verified live), DEGRADED (13 mis-tagged swing fills pending 22:00 UTC swing_cron + external limits + run-log gaps + trade 703 strategy=null + daytrading bar staleness).

## August 26, 2026 Control-104 _trigger regression fix (DEPLOYED)

Control-104 at ~02:00 UTC (Aug 26 04:00 +02). ## August 26, 2026 Control-101 strict read-only control + swing cap bypass fix (DEPLOYED)

Control-101 was a strict GET-only production control at ~23:00 UTC (Aug 26 01:00 +02). All six endpoints returned HTTP 200. A critical code defect was found and fixed: the daytrading final sync was re-attributing swing-owned positions to `strategy='daytrading'`, causing the swing cap check to see $0 exposure on the next swing run and bypass the `swing_max_capital_usd` $3,700 cap.

**Root cause:** `runTradingCycle` step 12 (index.ts ~line 1064) filtered broker positions with `!CRYPTO_SYMBOLS.has(p.symbol)` but did not exclude swing-tagged D1 positions. Every daytrading cycle reset all swing positions to `strategy='daytrading'`. When swing_cron ran at 22:00 UTC, it saw $0 swing exposure, approved 12 new BUY orders ($1,450 estimated), and re-attributed everything back to swing. This flip-flop meant the swing cap was never effectively enforced.

**Evidence:** Swing run 3574 (Aug 25 22:01:33 UTC, status=skipped, errors=0, 40311ms) placed 12 new swing BUY orders (trades 708-719, all status=accepted, day-TIF). Positions flipped from "all daytrading" (Control-100) to "all swing" (MV $7,951, over the $3,700 cap). The 12 orders remain accepted and could fill at Aug 26 market open.

**Fix deployed:** Commit `a206690`. The daytrading final sync now excludes swing-tagged D1 positions (`swingOwnedSymbols` set) before upserting, preserving strategy ownership across cycles. 220 tests / 822 assertions, typecheck clean. No caps changed. Deployed via direct Cloudflare API PUT (HTTP 200, success=true). Post-deploy GET verification confirmed /health=2.6.0, config.version=2.6.0, all endpoints HTTP 200.

**Live risk (requires Joachim decision):** 12 pending swing BUY orders (trades 708-719, day-TIF, status=accepted) could fill at Aug 26 09:30 ET market open, pushing swing exposure to ~$9,400 (2.5x the $3,700 cap). These orders were placed by the bot under the now-fixed defect. Cancelling them requires a broker mutation (not performed during read-only control). Joachim should decide whether to cancel before market open.

**Version identity:** /health=2.6.0, release_version=2.6.0, config.version=2.6.0 aligned. HEAD `a206690` (code+docs). Code commit `a206690`. 220 tests / 822 assertions, typecheck clean.

**Status: HEALTHY** (code/deployment — cap bypass defect fixed and deployed). **DEGRADED** (12 pending swing BUY orders risk cap overshoot at market open; external data-feed/resource limits; run-log delivery gaps; CYCLE_LEASE_HELD streaks from free-tier subrequest/CPU exhaustion).

**Corrections (code commit `22e962f`):** (1) `accounting_status` now distinguishes `no_fill` (zero-fill accepted orders) from `filled_lot_exact_unavailable` (filled but no lot-exact gross/fee/net), fixing the Control-86 conflation; (2) terminal `done_for_day`/`stopped` orders with partial fills are classified `executed=1` instead of `executed=2`, preserving partial-fill evidence; (3) equity-direction fallback (`accountWithEquityDirection`) now applied to the `/api/dashboard` account object, not just `/api/account`. **220 tests / 822 assertions** pass; TypeScript and `git diff --check` clean. No caps, order placement, cancellation, reconciliation-write, or trading-behavior change.

The exact current code-bearing checkout is branch `fix/remove-premature-position-upsert-entryside`, code commit `22e962fac86e2e4cb2f0679fa1745992c0eae8d8`, release **2.6.0**. Deployed via direct Cloudflare API PUT with GET-only post-deploy verification. Remaining follow-ups: D1-seeded `config.version` sync to 2.6.0, natural swing run recovery from subrequest exhaustion, crypto bar freshness investigation, `rawEdgeBps` producer for crypto edge gate, D1 free-tier optimization before Sep 1, and plan upgrade decision.

## August 25, 2026 Control-90 D1 free-tier optimization - LIVE VERIFIED

Control-90 deployed D1 free-tier optimizations to stay within the 5M rows read / 100k rows written daily limits before Sep 1 enforcement. Code commit `62ff44f` plus fee-cache-refresh fix in HEAD `6876a92`. Changes: (1) removed syncBrokerLedger from daytrading cycle (eliminated ~108 redundant sync invocations/day), (2) added `broker_ledger_synced_until` watermark shrinking overlap from 3 days to 15 min, (3) batched upsertBrokerActivities with D1 `batch()` instead of per-row `.run()`, (4) added indexes `broker_fees(order_id)`, `broker_fees(fee_type, created_date)`, `broker_fills(symbol, transaction_time)`, (5) cached `getBrokerFeeSummary` in `bot_config` so crypto strategy and dashboard use cached version instead of full-table scans 48x/day + every dashboard load, (6) maintenance cycle refreshes the fee summary cache every 10 min after ledger sync, (7) LIMIT 500 on `getStrategyComparison` time-series (was unbounded), (8) daily retention pruning for run_log (30d), snapshots/decisions/broker tables (90d). No caps, order semantics, or trading behavior changed. 220 tests / 822 assertions pass. Deployed via direct Cloudflare API PUT at 12:04:59 UTC (deployment_id `639c65b5`). Post-deploy GET-only verification: `/health`=2.6.0, run 3498 at 12:11 UTC `reconcile_cron status=ok errors=0`, equity $98,535.09, 21 broker-authoritative positions, caps $5,000/$3,700/$2,000 unchanged. Filtered observability confirmed: `code=MAINTENANCE_ONLY` and `status=filled`/`status=accepted` filters return correct results; `accounting_status` distinguishes `no_fill` from `filled_lot_exact_unavailable`.
